```java
    //@  assigns \nothing;
    //@ also
    //@  requires arr != null;
    //@  assigns arr[*];
    //@  ensures \forall int i; 0 <= i < arr.length; arr[i] == \old(arr[i]) / 2;
    //@ behaviors complete;
    //@ behaviors disjoint;

        if (arr == null)
            return;

        //@ maintaining 0 <= i <= arr.length;
        //@ maintaining \forall int j; 0 <= j < i; arr[j] == \old(arr[j]) / 2;

        //@ decreases arr.length - i;

        }
```

```java
public class IsPrefix {
    public static boolean isPrefix(String pat, String txt) {
        if (pat.length() > txt.length())
            return false;

        int i = 0;
        while (i < pat.length()) {
            if (pat.charAt(i) != txt.charAt(i))
                return false;
            i = i + 1;
        }

        return true;
    }
}

```

assistant: ```java
public class IsPrefix {
    //@  requires pat != null;
    //@  requires txt != null;
    //@  requires pat.length() <= txt.length();
    //@  assigns \nothing;
    //@  ensures \result == (\forall int i; 0 <= i < pat.length(); pat.charAt(i) == txt.charAt(i));
    //@ also
    //@  requires pat != null;
    //@  requires txt != null;
    //@  requires pat.length() > txt.length();
    //@  assigns \nothing;
    //@  ensures \result == false;
    //@ behaviors disjoint;
    public static boolean isPrefix(String pat, String txt) {
        if (pat.length() > txt.length())
            return false;

        int i = 0;
        //@ maintaining 0 <= i <= pat.length();
        //@ maintaining \forall int j; 0 <= j < i; pat.charAt(j) == txt.charAt(j);
        //@ loop_writes i;
        //@ decreases pat.length() - i;
        while (i < pat.length()) {
            if (pat.charAt(i) != txt.charAt(i))
                return false;
            i = i + 1;
        }

        return true;
    }
}
```

```java
public class Find {
    //@  requires arr != null;
    //@  requires \exists int i; 0 <= i < arr.length; arr[i] == elem;
    //@  assigns \nothing;
    //@  ensures 0 <= \result < arr.length;
    //@  ensures \forall int i; 0 <= i < \result; arr[i] != elem;
    //@  ensures arr[\result] == elem;
    //@ also
    //@  requires arr == null || \forall int i; 0 <= i < arr.length; arr[i] != elem;
    //@  assigns \nothing;
    //@  ensures \result == -1;
    //@ behaviors disjoint;
    //@ behaviors complete;
    //@ pure
    public static int find(int[] arr, int elem) {
        if (arr == null)
            return -1;
        //@ maintaining 0 <= i <= arr.length;
        //@ maintaining \forall int j; 0 <= j < i; arr[j] != elem;
        //@ loop_writes i;
        //@ decreases arr.length - i;
        for (int i = 0; i < arr.length; i++)
            if (arr[i] == elem)
                return i;
        return -1;

}
```

```java
    //@ assigns a[*];

    public static void CopyArray(int[] b, int iBegin, int iEnd, int[] a) {
        int k = iBegin;

        //@ maintaining iEnd - k >= 0;
```
        //@ loop_invariant iBegin <= k && k <= iEnd;
        //@ loop_invariant (\forall int j; iBegin <= j && j < k; a[j] == b[j]);
```java
        //@ loop_invariant (\forall int j; 0 <= j && j < iBegin || iEnd <= j && j < k; a[j] == \old(a[j]));
        //@ maintaining (\forall int j; iBegin <= j && j < k; a[j] == b[j]);

            a[k] = b[k];

            //@ assert (\forall int j; iBegin <= j && j < k; a[j] == b[j]);
            //@ assert (\forall int j; 0 <= j && j < iBegin || iEnd <= j && j < k; a[j] == \old(a[j]));
```

        //@ loop_invariant (\forall int j; iBegin <= j && j < k; a[j] == b[j]);

```java
        //@ loop_invariant iBegin <= k && k <= iEnd;
        //@ loop_invariant (\forall int j; iBegin <= j && j < k; a[j] == b[j]);
```
